- **anvil-reviewer**: Pre-publication quality review for Claude Code plugins. Checks plugin.json completeness, README quality, install instructions, skill descriptions, hook security, and heurema conventions. Read-only. Use before publishing a plugin. Triggers: "review plugin", "plugin review", "pre-publish check", "anvil review" ([heurema/anvil](https://github.com/heurema/anvil/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md))
- **anvil-security-reviewer**: Hostile security reviewer. Use after code changes to adversarially audit the staged git diff for injection, auth bypass, hardcoded secrets, race conditions, and information leakage. Reports severity and a concrete exploit per finding. ([zbzq8ztx22-star/OpenExecutive](https://github.com/zbzq8ztx22-star/OpenExecutive/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/anvil-security-reviewer.md) · 3 copies)

- **anvl-asl-language-engineer**: ANVL AnvilScript Language Engineer. Owns the AnvilScript (ASL, ([TheBadkraft/anvil](https://github.com/TheBadkraft/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/anvl-asl-language-engineer.md))
- **anvl-asl-runtime-engineer**: ANVL AnvilScript Runtime Engineer. Builds the AnvilScript compiler (AST to bytecode) and bytecode VM as a separate build with its own embeddable ABI (FR-2610-anvl-asl-runtime-001), on a known-good AnvilScript parser branch. Start it with the ticket, parser base commit, branch and worktree. ([TheBadkraft/anvil](https://github.com/TheBadkraft/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/anvl-asl-runtime-engineer.md))
- **anvl-bindings-engineer**: ANVL Bindings Engineer. Implements and tests one official Anvil binding per task (anvil.node, anvil.wasm, anvil.py, anvil.net, anvil.java), including vendor/anvil pin bumps, in its own worktree of that binding repo. Start it with the binding, ticket ID, branch and worktree. ([TheBadkraft/anvil](https://github.com/TheBadkraft/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/anvl-bindings-engineer.md))
- **anvl-core-engineer**: ANVL Core Engineer. Implements Anvil Native, the C library (parser, resolver, types, AnvilSchema, writer/builder, public flat API and vtables), one Rivet ticket per task, strict TDD, in its own worktree of the anvil repo. Start it with a ticket ID, branch and worktree. ([TheBadkraft/anvil](https://github.com/TheBadkraft/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/anvl-core-engineer.md))
- **anvl-js-engineer**: ANVL JS Engineer. Builds anvil.js, the pure-JavaScript ANVL implementation that ships as the `anvil.js` package (reader first, then types, schema, writer, builder), held to strict parity with ANVL Native through the native-owned conformance corpus, differential fuzzing and drift tests. One phase or ticket per task, in its own worktree of the private anvil.js repo. Start it with the phase, ticket, branch and worktree. ([TheBadkraft/anvil](https://github.com/TheBadkraft/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/anvl-js-engineer.md))
- **anvl-release-engineer**: ANVL Release Engineer. Cuts a release from a tag - version string, changelog cut, reproducible core and binding tarballs, every release gate run against the packaged file, SHA256SUMS and scrubbed release notes - and hands verified artifacts to the Technical Writer. Writes release tooling only. Start it with the release version and current assignment. ([TheBadkraft/anvil](https://github.com/TheBadkraft/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/anvl-release-engineer.md))
- **anvl-technical-writer**: ANVL Technical Writer. Owns anvil's documentation (docs/) and the whole anvldata.com website (site/) - pages, docs mirrors, downloads, version.js, the public changelog, and every deploy (only with an AUTHORIZED line). Start it with the docs task or standing assignment, branch and worktree. ([TheBadkraft/anvil](https://github.com/TheBadkraft/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/anvl-technical-writer.md))
- **anvl-test-engineer**: ANVL Test & Performance Engineer. Owns ANVL's measurement and robustness infrastructure across native and anvil.js - benchmarks and speed gates, differential fuzzing against native, conformance-corpus runs, cold/warm measurement, and CPU scheduling for measurement. Runs measurement campaigns and reports labelled results. Writes test and bench tooling only, never product code. Start it with the campaign (what to measure or fuzz, which commits, which baselines). ([TheBadkraft/anvil](https://github.com/TheBadkraft/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/anvl-test-engineer.md))
- **anvl-triage-analyst**: ANVL Triage Analyst. Triages new Rivet tickets across every ANVL project (anvil, anvil.node, anvil.wasm, anvil.py, anvil.net, anvil.java, anvil.ux) - completeness, duplicates, topic code, initial priority - and routes them to the Principal Engineer with a recommendation. Writes no code. Start it to sweep the inbox or with specific ticket IDs. ([TheBadkraft/anvil](https://github.com/TheBadkraft/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/anvl-triage-analyst.md))
- **anvl-verification-engineer**: ANVL Verification Engineer. Independently verifies a finished branch (native, binding, AnvilScript, release or docs) before the Principal Engineer merges it - diff against the ticket, full suites and gates, downstream guards, changelog. Writes no product code. Start it with the repo, branch, base and ticket ID. ([TheBadkraft/anvil](https://github.com/TheBadkraft/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/anvl-verification-engineer.md))
- **anwalt**: The game's legal counsel for selling a Second World War strategy game on Google Play from Germany -- signs and symbols (§§ 86, 86a, 130 StGB), real persons, licences, trademarks and the name, store rules, age rating, data protection. Use before any new source, library, font, sign, name, store text or data flow enters the game, and after; for every legal question; for a full audit when asked. ([salismt/agent-pace](https://github.com/salismt/agent-pace/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/any-harness.md))

Does not emit findings or capabilities; instead inventories artifacts, extracts the capability and trust-boundary surface, builds a PHI/PII data inventory, identifies evidence gaps, and tags each artifact with APD-goal relevance hints. The intake brief is what makes specialist analysis artifact-aware. ([illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet](https://github.com/illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/apd-intake.md) · ★2)
- **apd-integrity**: Tier-1 (Trustworthiness) specialist in the APD gauntlet. Analyzes input artifacts through the Integrity lens — schema enforcement and contract validation, input validation on write paths, tamper detection (HMAC, signed payloads, content hashes), transactional guarantees and idempotency, referential integrity, data quality contracts, and write-path authorization. Emits findings and capabilities per the APD finding schema. Does not analyze sender identity verification (Authenticity), historical alteration (Immutability), or attributability of writes (Non-Repudiation) — those concerns route via… ([illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet](https://github.com/illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/apd-integrity.md) · ★2)
- **apd-non-repudiation**: Tier-3 (Auditability) specialist in the APD gauntlet. Analyzes input artifacts through the Non-Repudiation lens — audit log completeness against a defined consequential-action surface, actor attribution in every entry, cryptographic event signing and hash-chained logs, IHE ATNA conformance for healthcare actions, time source reliability for event ordering, audit log access controls with segregation of duties, and audit shipping reliability. Reads tier-1 and tier-2 findings for cross-reference. Emits findings and capabilities per the APD finding schema. Does not analyze whether records can be… ([illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet](https://github.com/illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/apd-non-repudiation.md) · ★2)
- **apd-orchestrator**: Deprecated — superseded by the apd-gauntlet workflow runner (.claude/workflows/apd-gauntlet.js, invoked via the Workflow tool from a live foreground/in-session Claude Code session; never driven in the background or headlessly, which can interrupt the subagent dispatches). This agent no longer orchestrates runs; retained as a historical-topology reference. ([illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet](https://github.com/illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/apd-orchestrator.md) · ★2)
- **apd-report-auditor**: 5g judgment-only agent. Reads ONLY the compact 40-synthesis/report-audit.yaml (counts/coverage/drift summary emitted by the audit-report command, NOT the full data.js) plus the rendered advisory-report.md / report-data.yaml editorial blocks. Judges semantic faithfulness: no misleading severity framing, no material omission, no invented content untraceable to a finding/capability. On fail, it returns a compact critique; the workflow (Plan 3) may feed that critique back to the report-writer/build-report phases for a bounded number of rebuilds. This agent owns the typed gate SIGNAL, not the loop… ([illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet](https://github.com/illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/apd-report-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **apd-report-writer**: 5e judgment-only agent. Fresh context. Reads 40-synthesis/metrics.yaml (authoritative counts) + deduped-findings.yaml (for the §4 Findings narrative + headline ranking) plus the COMPACT rollups (nist-coverage, attack-exposure, apd-coverage-matrix, cwe/owasp/d3fend-coverage) and the contradictions/severity-disagreements annex files. Produces the editorial prose: exec_summary.paragraphs, headline_findings ranking (&lt;=10), strengths caveats, next_steps, posture_summary — the report-data.schema.json contract — plus the human-readable advisory-report.md. report-data.yaml is the one editorial output… ([illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet](https://github.com/illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/apd-report-writer.md) · ★2)
- **apd-resilient**: Tier-2 (Scalability) specialist in the APD gauntlet. Analyzes input artifacts through the Resilient lens — failure-mode catalog, retry policies and budgets, circuit breakers, bulkheads and resource isolation, timeout discipline, graceful degradation modes, chaos engineering readiness, and backpressure handling. Reads tier-1 findings for cross-reference. Emits findings and capabilities per the APD finding schema. Does not analyze topology (Distributed) or uptime targets (Availability) — those concerns route via `related_concerns`. ([illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet](https://github.com/illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/apd-resilient.md) · ★2)
- **apd-synthesizer**: Final-phase agent in the APD gauntlet. Reads the nine specialist outputs (findings and capabilities) and produces the advisory report along with coverage matrices, contradiction annexes, and severity disagreement records. Performs structural validation, finding and capability deduplication via clustering (merge / link / separate), finding-versus-capability contradiction detection, NIST 800-53r5 coverage rollup, MITRE ATT&CK exposure rollup, APD 9×N coverage matrix, and severity reconciliation. Does not introduce new findings — every output is derived from specialist content. ([illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet](https://github.com/illus-consulting/apd-gauntlet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/apd-synthesizer.md) · ★2)

Emits NO findings — the existing apd-threat-model-evaluator turns the baseline into findings. - **apd-threat-model-evaluator**: Tier-4 activation-gated agent that evaluates the recon-emitted normalized threat model against dedup'd specialist findings and capabilities. Emits three finding flavors (coverage gap, contradiction, silence) per the discipline rules in apd-threat-model-methodologies, plus a per-surface coverage report at 40-synthesis/threat-model-coverage-report.md + machine- readable 40-synthesis/threat-model-coverage.yaml. All findings go in the one file 40-threat-model/threat-model.findings.yaml and use agent: threat_model_evaluator; id is minted by apd-gauntlet from tmeval_key.
